=ARRAYTOTEXT(CONCAT(TEXTAFTER(Data!Z9,"b")))
=TOROW(GESTEP({1,2,7;0.5,0,0.5}))
=BIN2DEC(ISODD(XOR(TRUNC(Data!I5))))
=TOROW(EDATE(MAXA(TRANSPOSE(REPT((1E+300),"1")),ROW(OFFSET(Data!J1:J5,2,0,3,2))),Data!N1:N3))
=UPPER(ARABIC(FLOOR.MATH(#NULL!,"Hello")))
=TOROW(ERF(VALUE(1/0))+AGGREGATE(DAYS(1E+5,Data!L1:L3),ATAN2(Data!N3,Data!L1:N1),TRANSPOSE(Data!G1:H5)))
=TOROW(CHOOSEROWS(TAKE(CHOOSEROWS(SORT({2.5,5,0;3,10,"";TRUE,"#N/A",TRUE}),(-2),2),2,Data!A1:B3),ABS(Data!E2)))
=TOROW(SORT(HLOOKUP(Data!O3,{"$5","";TRUE,"5%";10,"0"},Data!A1:A10,Data!B3),{4;-1;5;2.5;4},IFS(Data!A3:C5,Data!B9)))
=TOROW(PROPER(CHOOSECOLS(VLOOKUP(TIMEVALUE(""),INDEX(Data!L1:N3,Data!A1:A5,2),AREAS(Data!B7),ISNONTEXT(LN(0))),EXP(Data!B4))),3)
=ROUND(1E-5,BITLSHIFT(Data!A10,PRODUCT(Data!B1:B5)))
=TOROW(VLOOKUP(5,CHOOSEROWS(XLOOKUP(#DIV/0!,Data!B1:B12,Data!A1:A5,(1E+21)),COUNTIFS(Data!B4:B9,Data!B9,Data!D1:D5)),FREQUENCY(Data!E1:E6,Data!J1:J3),ISNA({0,2,"1.5",2})))
=TOROW(Data!L2:N2^SUMX2MY2(WRAPCOLS({3,0,"a";7,2.5,0.5;FALSE,FALSE,TRUE},2),SEQUENCE(3)),1)
=ISFORMULA(Data!A3:C5)
=INDEX(LEFT(SEQUENCE(0,"1"),ISREF(Data!A1:A5)),2,1)
=TOROW(CHOOSECOLS(EXPAND({3,3;"1.5","1/1/2020";1,"a"},4,TRUE,Data!D10),(-1)))
=TOROW(TEXT(VLOOKUP(FALSE,Data!A1:D2,Data!D1:D5,FALSE),"[>10]0;0.0"))
=TOROW(COMPLEX(ROW(Data!C6),LOOKUP("1E2",Data!G1:H5)))
=TOROW(TEXTBEFORE(SWITCH(TEXTSPLIT(LOOKUP(Data!Z9,{"$5";TRUE},Data!B9:B12),ADDRESS(Data!A2,2,4,Data!B10),PROPER("é")),HLOOKUP(Data!C7,Data!C1:C5,4,TRUE),{4,"a","1E2",7;"a",3,"1.5",1}),Data!C1:C5,AGGREGATE(MIN(HSTACK(Data!O1:O3,Data!G2:H5),{10,TRUE,TRUE;"1.5",2,3;2,FALSE,10}),(-2),MOD(AND(Data!E1:E6,"(3)"),Data!L1:N3),TEXTSPLIT(#N/A,(2^53),"12abc")-SCAN(#NAME?,Data!A1:B3,LAMBDA(acc,v,acc)))))
=TOROW(SUMIFS(Data!A1:A3,IF(SORT(Data!B4:B9,3,2),Data!N1:N3,Data!A1:A10),LOOKUP(COLUMN(INDEX(Data!L1:L3,TIMEVALUE(Data!B11),Data!D10*Data!A3)),VSTACK(Data!A1:A5))),1)
=TOROW(HLOOKUP(UNICHAR(Data!D1),LOOKUP(Data!Z9,{2.5,2;2,"TRUE"},Data!Z1:Z3),CHAR(255)))
=INDEX(TRUNC(SWITCH(TOCOL(Data!D1:D5,1),WRAPROWS({"0",1,2,5},2),SUMIFS(Data!A1:D2,Data!A3:C5,TRUE),SLOPE(Data!A1:A5,Data!I1:I10),Data!A7,WRAPROWS(Data!E1:E6,1),COUNTBLANK(INDIRECT("Data!A1:A3")),CHOOSEROWS({"a",-1},FALSE())),Data!E1:E6),1,1)
=FIXED(IFS(FALSE,-1E-300))
=TOROW(CONCATENATE(TEXTBEFORE(CHOOSECOLS(DROP(Data!L1:N3,Data!N1:N3,3),Data!B7,Data!A8^Data!D2),LAMBDA(m,arr,CONCATENATE(32768,,(1E+10)))(OR({FALSE,"1/1/2020","5%"}),CHOOSECOLS(Data!Z1:AA2,{-1,0;2.5,-1;1,3})),SIGN(SWITCH({0,2.5,TRUE;"+3",1,3;-1,10,#NULL!},45000.75,Data!N1:N3,Data!Z1,Data!A4,0.30000000000000004,Data!A8,"$5")))))
=INDEX(ROW(Data!A6:Data!N3),1,2)
=TOROW(T.DIST(TRUE(),YEAR({-1,5,0;1,4,0;4,1,0.5}),Data!A1:B3))
=TOROW(MAKEARRAY(2,0,LAMBDA(i,j,MINUTE(TIME(INT(Data!D10),ISLOGICAL(Data!B6),FACTDOUBLE(5))))))
=CONCATENATE(Data!Z9)
=INDEX(Data!D1:D10,2,1)
=TOROW(DAYS360(EXP(Data!N1:N3),FALSE,{"a"}),1)
=INDEX(XLOOKUP("abc",Data!L1:L3,Data!I1:I10),Data!F5,XLOOKUP(Data!D1:D5,{2.5},Data!B9:B12))
=TOROW({#NAME?,2;-1,"a"})
=TOROW(WRAPROWS(COLUMN(Data!J1:J3),MATCH(1E-5,Data!Z1:AA2,Data!D1)))
="+3"
=(Data!L1-Data!D9)&FIXED(Data!D8)
=TOROW(XLOOKUP(Data!L1:N1,TOCOL(MINVERSE(Data!I1:I10)),IFS(ISEVEN({10,10;1,2.5;10,5}),DROP(Data!E1:E6,1),FALSE(),CONCATENATE("3","x y")),REPLACE(Data!D1:D10,ABS(#DIV/0!),Data!B7/Data!A7,SUBSTITUTE(#N/A,Data!F3,#DIV/0!,{-1,2.5,0;2,0.5,2.5;7,1,4}))))
=ROWS(Data!I1:I10)&"x"&COLUMNS(Data!I1:I10)
=TOROW(TEXT(Data!B9,CONCATENATE(Data!C1:C5,Data!B9,Data!Z1:AA2)))
=SIN(2^53)
